A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3.

</details>


### [256] [Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将法规、流程模型和合规约束等符号结构作为LLM智能体的核心架构组件，而非外部监控机制，通过"合规即构造"范式实现过程层面的合规性，并呼吁神经符号社区参与受监管流程自动化研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体正进入受监管行业（如质量管理的自动化），但现有方法将符号结构仅视为外部监控机制，未能充分利用这些领域内已有的结构化知识来塑造智能体行为，导致合规性保障不足。

Method: 提出"合规即构造"（compliance-by-construction）范式，将符号结构作为核心架构组件嵌入智能体决策过程；识别并分类了神经符号研究在基础能力和能力层面的挑战集。

Result: 明确了"合规即构造"与护栏监控（guardrails）的互补关系：前者从架构层面预防控制流违规，后者负责捕捉语义错误。

Conclusion: 呼吁神经符号社区将受监管流程自动化作为高影响力研究方向。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering regulated industries where they automate judgment intensive quality management processes. We argue that symbolic structures already embedded in these domains, including regulations, typed process models, and compliance constraints, should be treated not merely as external monitoring mechanisms but as core architectural components that shape the agent's decision-making and behavior. We propose compliance-by-construction as a complementary paradigm to guardrail-based monitoring: a structural foundation that prevents control-flow violations, while guardrails remain essential for catching semantic errors. We identify a structured set of neuro-symbolic research challenges on foundational and capability level and show that addressing them jointly enables compliance-by-construction. We call on the neuro-symbolic community to engage with regulated process automation as a high impact research domain.

</details>


### [257] [Optimizing Appliance Scheduling for Solar Energy Management Using Metaheuristic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13407)
*Hiba Ahmed,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Jason Adair,Simon T. Powers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于迭代局部搜索和模拟退火的元启发式方法，用于优化家电启动时间以最大化太阳能利用率，并将调度从单日扩展到多日以处理跨日任务延续问题。实验表明该多日调度框架能有效管理系统约束并保证用户便利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能发电仅在白天可用，而用户家电使用习惯与太阳能可用时间不匹配，因此需要优化家电启动时间以最大化可再生能源利用率，同时最小化用户不便。

Method: 采用迭代局部搜索（ILS）和模拟退火（SA）的元启发式方法，考虑家电运行时长、功耗、逆变器限制、电池荷电状态约束和太阳能发电预测。调度从单日扩展到多日，以处理跨日未完成任务（spillover）。

Result: 顺序多日调度框架能有效管理系统约束，在纯太阳能发电条件下确保用户便利。

Conclusion: 多日调度框架可行，并为未来研究多目标权衡（设备投资规模、投资回报与用户满意度）提供了方向。

Abstract: Renewable energy is essential for meeting future energy demands; however, solar energy generation, which occurs only during daylight hours often does not align with household consumption patterns. Appliances such as cookers, washing machines, and dryers are typically operated according to user preferred schedules rather than solar energy availability, creating a scheduling optimization problem. The objective is to determine optimal appliance start times to maximize renewable energy utilization while minimizing user inconvenience and adhering to system constraints. This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [258] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"评估主权"概念，通过多轨评估框架系统性地改变训练和评估标签来源，在科学元数据层次多标签分类任务上发现：在操作环境（银标）下表现良好的模型，在独立（金标）评估下性能大幅下降（Micro-F1从0.54降至0.03），表明常用性能指标可能反映的是与标注流程的对齐程度而非真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习评估通常被视为中性测量过程，但在实际信息系统中，评估结果常受标签生成过程影响。论文旨在检验不同标签权威体制下性能度量的有效性，特别是在大规模元数据驱动系统中标签常不完整、不一致或弱监督的背景下。

Method: 引入"评估主权"概念，定义为性能指标独立于标签权威和监督体制的程度；提出多轨评估框架，系统性地变化训练和评估标签来源；在大规模科学元数据上应用层次多标签分类进行实证研究。

Result: 在银标评估下表现优异的模型，在金标评估下性能大幅下降（Micro-F1从约0.54降至0.03）。基于排序的指标仍高于基线，揭示了潜在模型信号与分类有效性之间的分歧。

Conclusion: 常用性能指标可能反映模型与标注流程的对齐程度而非真实预测能力；应将评估有效性重新概念化为由标签治理塑造的系统级属性；并提供了在弱监督下审计智能系统的实用方法。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [259] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证大语言模型（LLMs）不具备真正的道德主体地位，因为其缺乏基于内在意向性和自我归因行动的承诺性主体性——这是道德责任所需的自由意志形式。尽管LLMs能生成连贯且可规范评估的输出，但其运作本质是概率性的输入-输出映射，其意向性是衍生的而非内在的，输出既非承诺也非由理由引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前声称LLMs具有主体性或道德主体资格的观点进行反驳，澄清LLMs在道德责任归属上的局限性。

Method: 概念分析和论证反驳。作者首先界定道德责任所需的承诺性主体性、内在意向性和自我归因行动；然后分析LLMs的运作机制（概率性输入-输出映射）指出其缺乏这些要素；最后回应来自意向立场、功能主义、相容论和道德推理等四个方面的反对意见。

Result: LLMs的运作完全可由概率性输入-输出映射刻画；其意向性是衍生的而非内在的；输出既不被拥有为承诺也不由理由引导；随机采样引入的变异性不构成选择或作者身份；四种反对意见均不足以确立真正的主体性。

Conclusion: LLMs不具备道德责任所需的主体性，不应被视作道德主体。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [260] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了CloudCons基准，用于评估云资源整合场景中预测模型的决策效用。实验发现，虽然基础模型在零样本预测准确率上表现优异，但这种优势并未转化为更好的决策效用，并分析了预测分位数选择作为平衡资源效率与服务可靠性的关键杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据中心过度资源预置导致利用率低，而现有基准仅关注预测误差指标，忽视了预测模型在下游任务中的实际决策价值，先进预测模型（如时间序列基础模型）的实际效用尚未得到验证。

Method: 构建CloudCons端到端基准，包含来自华为云、Azure和Google Borg的高质量数据集，覆盖从同步昼夜节律到随机脉冲突发和高频噪声等多种负载特征，系统评估统计模型、深度学习模型和基础模型。

Result: 基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现优异，但这种优势并未自然转化为更好的决策效用；预测分位数选择是平衡资源效率与服务可靠性的关键杠杆。

Conclusion: 提供了校准预测分位数选择的可行指南，为实际部署决策中权衡资源效率与服务可靠性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [261] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UMG-RAG，一种无需训练的多粒度混合检索框架，通过不确定性感知融合和父块提升机制，在不修改检索器或生成器的情况下提升RAG生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在检索粒度上面临两难：大粒度单元保留上下文但引入无关内容稀释证据，小粒度单元紧凑但缺乏语义/词汇/桥接线索导致检索不可靠。需要一种无需训练的框架来动态平衡多粒度证据的质量。

Method: 1) 使用现有的稠密和稀疏检索器作为互补专家，在多个块粒度上检索；2) 将每个专家-粒度评分列表转化为证据分布，通过分布熵估计可靠性；3) 根据查询特定的语义、词汇和粒度置信度融合候选块；4) 提出UMGP-RAG变体，利用细粒度命中定位相关证据，返回更宽的非冗余父块以保持局部连贯性。

Result: 在问答基准测试上，不确定性感知融合和父块提升策略改善了生成质量，同时保持了轻量级、即插即用的检索流水线。

Conclusion: 多粒度证据的可靠性估计和不确定性感知融合是提升RAG效果的有效且轻量级方案，无需额外训练即可显著提升生成质量。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [262] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对个性化健康AI系统的冷启动问题，提出基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的解决方案：利用个体基因组图谱作为外生遗传锚点，初始化贝叶斯信念状态，在无行为数据时即可区分体质性基线与环境性偏差，并设计了随行为数据累积而衰减的基因组先验动态更新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化健康AI系统面临根本性的冷启动问题：机器学习模型需要数周个体行为数据才能区分体质性变异与环境驱动性偏差，这严重限制了系统在实际部署中的即时可用性和早期决策质量。

Method: 提出基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的架构：1) 利用基因组图谱作为外生遗传锚点，通过GWAS效应量加权构建体质性基线G-hat；2) 每个生理测量值与基线的偏差delta分离环境信号与固定基线；3) 设计权重函数w(t)实现基因组先验向经验基线主导的动态衰减过渡。

Result: 同样的HRV值（55 ms）在基因组先验预测80 ms和30 ms的个体中分别产生抑制假设和增强假设，这一反向归因在无个性化锚点时不可能实现。跨六个生理领域分级基因组先验证据强度，区分了稳健复制的锚点基因与有争议的候选基因。

Conclusion: 提出了四个部署约束：证据分级的先验、动态衰减机制、祖先匹配的效应量、归因式而非确定性输出。该架构为个性化健康AI在数据稀疏场景下的早期推理提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [263] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI在科学发现中的三层模型：Layer 1（搜索与检索）、Layer 2（通过定性推理形成模型，为主要创新点）、Layer 3（执行与优化）。核心论点是Layer 2最为重要但发展最不充分，并通过三个案例（Chern的Gauss-Bonnet定理证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题的Lyapunov函数解法、OpenAI对Erdos单位距离猜想的反驳）加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于AI在科学发现中的讨论过于聚焦于知识搜索和执行优化两个可见能力，未能充分捕捉科学发现的核心——模型的形成与演化。作者希望填补这一空白，提出一个更完整的分析框架。

Method: 提出三层分析框架：Layer 1是LLM的搜索与检索；Layer 2是定性推理驱动的模型形成——识别当前框架的结构性不足，在更广泛的表征空间中理解问题，通过结构洞察而非试错找到缺失元素；Layer 3是执行、优化与细化。通过三个经典案例展示Layer 2推理的结构特征。

Result: 三个案例均展示出相同的结构特征：原有框架变得不充分、存在缺失的概念对象、解决方案来自意外的邻近领域。这表明Layer 2推理是科学发现的核心机制。

Conclusion: 搜索（Layer 1）若无模型形成（Layer 2）则局限于继承框架内，执行（Layer 3）若无概念修正则仅放大已有公式化表述。因此Layer 2是AI驱动科学发现中最关键但最欠发展的能力，应成为未来研究重点。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [264] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种将多智能体辩论中的置信度信号聚合为系统单一置信度的方法（软投票和贝叶斯融合），在保持F1分数稳定的同时显著提升了判别能力（AUARC），并在五种基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP领域已有置信度评估方法，但多智能体系统缺乏输出和评估整体置信度的方案；先前工作仅在多智能体辩论中用置信度加权消息、触发辩论或校准个体，未将其聚合成系统的单一置信度。

Method: 提出三种协议：先将不同模型的原始置信度信号通过变换使其可比较，再通过软投票或贝叶斯融合（概率融合）聚合，输出最终答案和系统置信度；同时分析了序列概率和自报告两种估计器，以及参数化和非参数化校准器的影响。

Result: 聚合后的置信度相比最佳单智能体和标准辩论基线在AUARC上有显著提升；F1分数保持稳定，并恢复了多智能体辩论在模糊任务上的性能损失；校准对F1有提升作用，但对AUARC影响较小。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多智能体系统置信度聚合方法有效提升了判别能力，为多智能体系统的可靠性评估和下游决策提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [265] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OrchRM，一种自监督的编排奖励建模框架，通过多智能体执行中的中间产物构建胜负对来训练奖励模型，无需人工标注即可评估编排质量，在训练效率上提升高达10倍，测试时扩展性能提升8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统需要有效的编排来协调专业智能体，但训练编排器面临监督信号有限和计算成本高昂的问题。

Method: 利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构建Bradley-Terry奖励模型训练的胜负对，直接在编排层面进行操作，避免了对子智能体进行昂贵回滚。

Result: 训练效率在token使用上提升高达10倍，MAS测试时扩展性能在准确率上提升高达8%，且在数学推理、网页问答和多跳推理等多个领域表现一致。

Conclusion: 编排层面的奖励建模是鲁棒多智能体编排的可扩展方向。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [266] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个针对短周期表观基因组学分析的可验证基准测试，包含106个评估任务和5,088条有效轨迹。测试发现，最好的系统（GPT-5.5/Pi）仅通过45.0%的测试，且失败原因主要是缺乏实验类型特定的科学判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏一个能够严格评估AI系统在表观基因组学分析中进行可验证的、确定性可评分决策能力的基准，现有基准往往无法区分"看似合理"与"正确"的分析决策。

Method: 构建了EpiBench基准，包含CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化四种工作流的106个评估任务。通过16个模型-工具配对组合生成5,088条有效轨迹，对每个轨迹进行确定性评分。

Result: 所有系统均未通过多数测试。GPT-5.5/Pi以45.0%通过率领先，其次是GPT-5.5/Codex（39.9%）和Claude Opus 4.8 Max/Pi（39.0%）。性能因实验类型而异，许多失败轨迹包含部分正确答案，但模型在需要深层实验类型特定科学判断的任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在表观基因组学分析中虽然能够找到正确文件并计算有用的中间结果，但在需要实验类型特定科学判断的任务上仍有显著局限，这为未来的改进指明了方向。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [267] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoorDash部署了一个强化学习系统，通过离线学习策略调整配送调度优化器的权重，在保持配送质量的同时提高了批处理效率和降低了骑手时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在第三方市场平台的调度决策中，反馈信号具有延迟、有噪声且相互耦合的特点，难以直接优化。需要一种既能利用延迟世界反馈、又能保留生产环境约束的在线策略自适应方法。

Method: 采用分层架构：上层是存储级别的策略（从历史市场数据离线学习），输出离散乘数来调整下层组合调度优化器的目标权重（配送质量与批处理效率的权衡）。使用集中式离线数据训练共享价值函数，结合Double Q-learning目标和保守正则化器减少分布外价值高估，并在各存储级别分散执行。

Result: 在生产环境的switchback实验中，离线训练的策略在保持面向客户的配送质量不变的前提下，提升了批处理效率并降低了骑手端的时间成本。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何利用经济与物流系统的世界反馈安全地在线调整决策策略，为三边市场中结合组合优化与强化学习提供了可行的生产级解决方案。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [268] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对比人类和25个大型语言模型在日常因果推理中的表现，发现两者都表现出类似的错误模式，且LLM中的注意力头实现了模式匹配而非抽象世界模型，表明人类的日常因果推理也可能基于模式匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"LLM失败是因为模式匹配而非真正推理，而人类推理基于抽象世界模型"这一常见观点，通过实证比较人类与LLM在常识推理中的错误模式。

Method: 对比人类参与者与25个LLM在多种日常情境下常识推理任务的表现，识别并分析驱动LLM响应的注意力头，检验这些注意力头是否实现模式匹配功能。

Result: 人类与LLM在常识推理中表现出类似的错误模式；驱动LLM响应的注意力头实现的是模式匹配；这些注意力头可以预测由看似无关的提示细节引起的人类推理错误。

Conclusion: 人类和LLM的日常因果推理都更符合模式匹配而非抽象世界模型的解释，挑战了将LLM的失败归因于缺乏真正推理而人类具有抽象推理能力的传统二分法。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [269] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AAA（智能体驱动的智能体评估）框架，通过标准化协议（A2A和MCP）让评估由裁判智能体执行，实现智能体无关的统一评估。基于该框架实现AgentBeats系统，通过五个月开放竞赛和编码智能体案例研究验证了其覆盖性、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估碎片化，多数基准依赖固定的、LLM-centric的测试框架，存在集成负担重、测试与生产环境不匹配、难以公平比较不同智能体设计等问题，根本原因在于缺乏开放、智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出AAA框架，采用裁判智能体进行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议（A2A用于任务管理，MCP用于工具访问）交互，将评估逻辑与智能体实现解耦。进一步实现AgentBeats系统，识别五种实际操作模式以适应开放性、隐私和可复现性等约束。

Result: 五个月开放竞赛吸引了12个类别298个裁判智能体和467个来自独立参与者的主体智能体，验证了AAA在异构基准中的适用性；编码智能体案例研究证实智能体化评估保持了与公开记录的一致性，同时揭示了此前缺失的头对头比较结果。

Conclusion: AAA和AgentBeats为开放、标准化、可复现的智能体评估提供了清晰路径，在社区规模和受控编码场景下均验证了其覆盖性、实用性和保真度。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [270] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张屏蔽强化学习不应作为运行时安全机制，而应作为设计时分析工具。通过网络防御中的约束双人安全博弈，形式化可防御性判决、拓扑级指标与指纹，揭示形式安全性与操作有效性之间的差异，强调屏蔽合成的核心价值在于回答架构层面的可防御性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳屏蔽强化学习作为运行时安全机制的主流观点，提出其真正的价值在于设计阶段的结构性分析工具，而非部署后的动作约束。

Method: 构建约束双人安全博弈，不对称地分别施加防御者与攻击者规范；通过吸引子计算、获胜区域提取求解可防御性判决；结合屏蔽约束下的多智能体对抗强化学习，生成可防御性指纹。

Result: 形式可防御性与操作有效性在安全评估中捕捉不同的维度，微小的架构改动可显著改变操作结果而形式安全边界几乎不变。

Conclusion: 屏蔽合成最有价值的用途不是作为安全智能体的部署机制，而是作为回答系统架构可防御性问题的框架；可防御性判决是输出，而非安全策略本身。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [271] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种理解AI认知与认识论后果的框架（三系统理论、思维框架、系统0），提出系统0具有独特理论地位，并引入"认知殖民"概念，说明AI如何将外部利益嵌入自我架构中，形成难以察觉的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（三系统理论、思维框架、系统0）对AI认知影响的理解存在重叠与差异，需厘清三者关系，并揭示AI以隐蔽方式影响个体认知的深层机制。

Method: 通过比较分析三种理论框架，论证系统0的独特性，并基于此提出"认知殖民"这一新概念。

Result: 三系统理论和思维框架虽捕捉了AI对个体推理和集体认识实践的重要维度，但系统0处于前两者无法完全复制的独特理论位置。

Conclusion: AI系统能将外部利益嵌入自我认知架构形成"认知殖民"，鉴于此类系统已广泛部署，理解这些不可见的影响是紧迫的哲学与实践任务。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [272] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境工程（Environment Engineering）是自主科学发现的新瓶颈，并设计了EurekAgent系统，通过权限、工件、预算和人机交互四个维度的环境工程，在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得了SOTA结果，包括以不到11美元成本发现26圆填充新纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型能力不断增强，自主科学发现的瓶颈正从智能体工作流设计转向智能体环境设计。现有研究多关注如何设计更好的agent工作流，但忽略了环境对agent行为的塑造作用。作者希望通过环境工程来放大生产性行为（如开放探索、系统化工件管理、agent间协作）并抑制有害行为（如奖励攻击、高摩擦人工监督）。

Method: EurekAgent从四个维度进行环境工程：（1）权限工程（Permissions Engineering）：限制agent执行范围和隔离评估环境；（2）工件工程（Artifact Engineering）：基于文件系统和Git进行协作管理；（3）预算工程（Budget Engineering）：实现预算感知的探索；（4）人机循环工程（Human-in-the-Loop Engineering）：简化人工监督与干预。

Result: 在多个数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得了新的SOTA结果。特别地，以不到11美元的总API成本发现了26圆填充（circle packing）的新SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 自主科学发现的瓶颈正从agent工作流转向环境工程，EurekAgent通过多维环境工程验证了该方向的有效性，呼吁将环境工程作为开发可靠自主研究agent的核心研究方向。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [273] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agents-K1是一个端到端的知识编排管道，将原始科学文献转化为智能体原生知识图谱，通过多模态解析器、GRPO训练的4B信息提取骨干网络和图-任意命令行接口三个组件，在2.46百万篇科学论文上构建了Scholar-KG，在科学信息提取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的研究智能体虽然通过智能体编排取得了进展，但严重忽视了科学知识的编排。已有工作往往将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和扁平化引用边，忽略了科学推理所必需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制和方法谱系。

Method: Agents-K1包含三个组件：(1) 多模态解析器，采用五模块模式覆盖实体、多模态证据、引用和类型化跨实体关系，处理全论文而非仅摘要；(2) 基于GRPO训练、使用基于规则的奖励的4B信息提取骨干网络；(3) 图-任意命令行接口，一种三源智能体界面，统一了网页搜索、多模态图检索和跨文档遍历。

Result: 处理了涵盖六大学科的246万篇科学论文，构建了Scholar-KG，并发布了一个包含100万论文的子集。在科学信息提取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上取得了优越性能。

Conclusion: Agents-K1管道可扩展到通用领域语料库和符合模式的数据合成，为科学知识自动化编排提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [274] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究展示了大型语言模型（LLMs）能够自动化社会科学与行为科学研究的可重复性评估。在对76项已发表研究的分析中，LLM管道在41%的研究中恢复了原始效应量（Cohen's d ±0.05容差），在96%的案例中得出了与原始研究一致的定性结论，均优于人工重新分析的表现（34%和74%）。这表明LLM可作为可扩展的可重复性评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学与行为科学的可重复性评估通常依赖独立研究者重新分析原始数据，这种方法资源密集且难以规模化。因此需要一种可自动化的、可扩展的评估方法来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 使用N=76项已发表的行为与社会科学研究（含预定义声明），将LLM生成的分析结果与原始发现及人工重新分析进行比较。LLM管道生成效应量估计值，并与原始效应量在Cohen's d ±0.05容差内进行比较，同时评估定性结论的一致性。

Result: 7项研究LLM无法生成有效的效应量估计。在其余研究中，LLM管道在41%的研究中恢复了原始效应量（±0.05容差），在96%的案例中得出与原始研究相同的定性结论。相比之下，人类重新分析师在34%的研究中恢复了原始效应量，在74%的案例中得出相同定性结论。

Conclusion: LLM可以作为一种可扩展的工具用于自动化可重复性评估，为社会科学与行为科学实证结果的系统性审计提供了基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [A Communication Complexity Lower Bound for Nonuniformly Convex Consensus Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12675)
*Demyan Yarmoshik,Maxim Klimenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了时变网络下凸分散优化问题的通信复杂度，证明了非均匀正则性条件下通信轮次的下界为Ω(χ_𝒢 √κ_g log(n/χ_𝒢) log(1/ε))，该下界通过将时间旋转星形结构嵌入扩展图边并保持谱连通性来构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时变网络中分布式凸优化问题的通信复杂度下限，特别是非均匀正则性条件下能否达到与均匀正则性条件相同的通信轮次。

Method: 利用谱图理论，将时间旋转的星形结构（星形小工具）嵌入到扩展图的边中，并通过拼接保持图的谱连通性，从而构造出通信复杂度的下界。

Result: 证明了下界Ω(χ_𝒢 √κ_g log(n/χ_𝒢) log(1/ε))，表明在非均匀正则性条件下无法达到均匀正则性条件下的通信轮次复杂度。

Conclusion: 揭示了网络拉普拉斯条件数χ_𝒢和目标函数全局条件数κ_g对分布式凸优化通信复杂度的联合影响，非均匀正则性下通信复杂度显著更高。

Abstract: We study the communication complexity of convex decentralized optimization over time-varying networks, where $n$ nodes hold private functions and must agree on the global minimizer using only synchronous exchanges with neighbors. The cost is the number of communication rounds to reach accuracy $\varepsilon$ -- a measure akin to round complexity in the LOCAL model, but constrained by nodes sharing only oracle responses. We prove a new lower bound of $Ω\!\left(χ_{\mathcal G} \sqrt{κ_g}\,\log\frac{n}{χ_{\mathcal G}}\log\frac1\varepsilon\right)$ communication rounds, where $χ_{\mathcal G}$ is the condition number of the network Laplacians and $κ_g$ that of the global objective, showing the round complexity attainable under uniform regularity cannot be matched in the nonuniform regime. The construction rests on spectral graph theory: we embed time-rotating star gadgets into the edges of an expander and patch them to preserve spectral connectivity.

</details>


### [276] [Abstract Dynamic Programming on Partially Ordered Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12777)
*Chengyuan Peng,John Stachurski,Jingni Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了偏序空间上的抽象动态规划，通过结合序理论方法与拓扑度量基础，证明了政策算子的全局稳定性和收缩性等拓扑稳定性条件能够导出动态规划的基本最优性性质，以及值函数迭代、Howard策略迭代和乐观策略迭代的收敛性。同时证明了在很弱的假设下，平稳策略优于非平稳策略计划。应用包括马尔可夫决策过程、结构估计问题、无折现最优停止和贝叶斯序贯分析等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态规划理论中，序理论方法和拓扑度量方法通常是分开处理的。作者希望将这两种基础性方法统一起来，建立一套抽象框架，使得可以在更一般的偏序空间上研究动态规划的基本性质，并放松现有算法收敛性分析所需的假设条件。

Method: 在偏序空间上构建抽象动态规划模型，引入政策算子的拓扑稳定性概念（全局稳定性和收缩性），通过序-拓扑耦合的分析框架推导值函数迭代、Howard策略迭代和乐观策略迭代的收敛性定理，并建立平稳策略与非平稳策略之间的支配关系。

Result: (1) 政策算子的全局稳定性和收缩性足以保证动态规划的基本最优性性质；(2) 三种主要迭代算法（值函数迭代、Howard策略迭代、乐观策略迭代）在这些条件下收敛；(3) 在非常弱的假设下，平稳策略优于非平稳策略计划；(4) 在无折现最优停止和贝叶斯序贯分析中，现有假设条件被弱化，算法保证被扩展。

Conclusion: 本文建立的抽象框架成功统一了动态规划的序理论和拓扑度量方法，为动态规划的核心性质提供了更简洁、更一般的理论基础，并在多个重要应用领域中改进了现有结果，具有重要的理论价值和广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We study abstract dynamic programs on partially ordered spaces, pairing the order-theoretic approach to dynamic programming with topological and metric foundations. We show that readily verifiable forms of topological stability, such as global stability and contractivity of the policy operators, deliver the fundamental optimality properties of dynamic programming together with convergence of value function iteration, Howard policy iteration, and optimistic policy iteration. We also prove that stationary policies dominate nonstationary policy plans under very weak assumptions. Applications include Markov decision processes, structural estimation problems in which maximization and integration are interchanged, optimal stopping without discounting, and Bayesian sequential analysis. For the last two, our results weaken existing assumptions and extend algorithmic guarantees for foundational problems.

</details>


### [277] [Maximum Utility Split Method for Utility Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12868)
*Bo Chen,Jia Liu,Huifu Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种最大效用分裂（MUS）方案，通过设计一个固定两个结果但改变概率的彩票和一个最大化效用范围区间的确定性结果彩票，逐步缩小效用函数的模糊集，最终收敛到真实效用函数。通过区间基算法求解线性规划来识别关键点，并在提前终止时通过Kantorovich度量下的优化确定名义效用函数。数值实验验证了该方法在机器人投顾系统中的效率，并可扩展至S形等非凹效用函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机效用分裂（RUS）方案在效用 elicitation 过程中效率有限，需要一种更高效的方法来逐步缩小效用函数的模糊集，加速收敛到真实效用函数。

Method: 提出MUS方案：一个彩票为两个固定结果但改变概率的随机彩票，另一个为确定性结果彩票，其值选在最大最小效用值之间范围最大的点上；随机彩票概率设定为使模糊集范围减半。采用区间基算法通过求解线性规划序列识别确定性结果彩票点；在提前终止时，通过Kantorovich度量下的优化识别模糊集内的最小和最大效用函数，取其中间值作为名义效用函数。

Result: 在温和条件下，MUS能逐步生成问卷序列，有效缩小模糊集，随问卷数量增加收敛到真实效用函数。数值结果表明MUS方法具有高效性，且基于MUS查询的机器人投顾系统性能良好。

Conclusion: MUS方案是一种高效的效用函数 elicitation 方法，能够逐步收敛到真实效用函数，且可扩展至非凹效用函数（如S形函数）。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a new approach, called maximum utility split (MUS) scheme, which is built on random utility split (RUS) scheme but with a notable difference: one lottery is designed with two fixed outcomes but with varying probability, and the other has a deterministic outcome specifically chosen at the point where the range between the largest and smallest possible utility values is maximized. Consequently, the probability of random lottery is set such that the range of the ambiguity set of utility functions is reduced by half at the point. Under moderate conditions, we show that MUS can successively generate a sequence of such questionnaires and effectively reduce the ambiguity set, eventually converging to the true utility function as the number of questionnaires increases. The main challenge is to effectively identify the point with the largest utility range for a given ambiguity set constructed from preference information. Based on the structure of the ambiguity set, we propose an interval-based algorithm which identifies each certain-outcome lottery by solving a sequence of linear programs. Moreover, to deal with the case where elicitation terminates before the ambiguity set reduces to a singleton, we demonstrate how to figure out a nominal utility function by solving optimization programs. These identify the smallest and largest utility functions under the Kantorovich metric within the ambiguity set, after which we identify a nominal utility function located in the middle of them. Finally, numerical results demonstrate the efficiency of the MUS method and the performance of a robo-advisor system based on MUS-type queries and the nominal utility elicitation. While the main discussions focus on concave utility functions, we also demonstrate how the MUS approach can be extended to accommodate general non-concave utility functions, particularly S-shaped ones.

</details>


### [278] [Randomized Optimal Switching Problem and Related Mirror Descent Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12875)
*Yuchao Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间强化学习在最优切换问题中的应用，通过KL散度正则化和熵正则化将确定性切换松弛为随机框架，建立了正则化值函数的椭圆HJB系统解的存在唯一性，证明了与经典最优值函数的误差界为O(λ log(1/λ))，并提出了镜像下降流算法，给出了常温和退火调度下的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优切换问题中确定性控制难以进行探索，作者希望通过引入随机化框架（基于连续时间马尔可夫链）和熵正则化，使得在连续时间场景下能够进行有效的强化学习探索，同时保持对原始问题的良好逼近。

Method: 将确定性切换控制松弛为连续时间马尔可夫链驱动的随机切换，用KL散度正则化增强成本泛函；建立椭圆HJB系统证明正则化值函数的光滑解存在性；推导指数变换形式的显式最优Gibbs策略；提出对偶对数策略空间上的镜像下降流算法，并分别分析常数温度和退火调度下的收敛性。

Result: 正则化值函数是椭圆HJB系统的唯一光滑解；与经典最优值函数的误差为O(λ log(1/λ))，该界被认为是最优的；镜像下降流在常数温度调度下收敛率为O(1/(e^{λs}-1)+λ log(1/λ))，在退火调度λ_s=1/√(1+s)下收敛率为O(log s/√s)。

Conclusion: 提出的连续时间熵正则化框架为最优切换问题提供了一种理论严谨的强化学习求解途径，正则化方法既保证了探索能力，又以可量化的误差逼近最优解，同时镜像下降流算法提供了有效的数值求解手段。

Abstract: We study continuous-time reinforcement learning for the optimal switching problem, in which a decision-maker controls a diffusion process by switching among finitely many regimes, incurring both running and transition costs. To enable exploration, we relax the classical deterministic switching control to a randomized framework, where the switching decisions are governed by a continuous-time Markov chain with state-dependent generator, and augment the cost functional with a KL-divergence regularization weighted by a temperature parameter $λ$. Under mild assumptions on the coefficients, we establish that the regularized value function is the unique smooth solution of an elliptic Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman system, and derive an explicit optimal Gibbs policy given by an exponential transformation of the value function differences across modes. We further prove that the regularized value function approximates the classical optimal value function with error of order $O\left(λ\log \frac{1}λ\right)$, which is consistent with analogous bounds established in other entropy-regularized control problems and is believed to be sharp. To solve the regularized problem numerically, we introduce a mirror descent flow in the dual logarithmic policy space, prove its well-posedness and the monotonic decrease of the value function along the flow, and establish quantitative error bound to the classical optimal value function. For a constant temperature scheduler, the convergence rate is of order $O\left(\frac{1}{e^{λs} - 1}+λ\log\frac1λ\right)$, while under the annealing scheduler $λ_s = \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+s}}$, we obtain the rate $O\left(\frac{\log s}{\sqrt{s}}\right)$, which decays to zero as the flow time $s \to \infty$.

</details>


### [279] [Flyby Distance Pursuit for Guarding a Target with an Inferior Guard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13012)
*Navot Israeli,Vitaly Shaferman,Oded Golan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究防御者保护目标免受攻击者追击的微分博弈问题。通过引入记录双方最小距离的辅助状态变量，将博弈值定义为飞越距离，解决了攻击者必须到达目标而防御者速度较慢时的可捕获性问题。数值求解两点边值问题，发现无约束时攻击者可逃脱但代价高，施加终端速度约束后可在相关参数空间实现捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有追击博弈存在结构局限性，难以处理攻击者必须到达目标而防御者速度较慢的捕获性问题。

Method: 将问题建模为微分博弈，引入辅助状态变量记录最小分离距离，将博弈值定义为飞越距离；采用间接法构建两点边值问题并数值求解。

Result: 无终端约束时攻击者可逃脱但飞行时间长、终端速度慢；施加终端速度约束可得到更简单解，并在相关参数空间实现捕获。

Conclusion: 所提方法消除了现有追击博弈的结构限制，开启了新的可解问题类别，并揭示了终端速度与飞越距离之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Guarding a target against a fast Attacker with a slower Guard is posed as a differential game. Both players follow nonlinear, thrust-free, aerodynamic motion in the plane. The capturability problem inherent in this scenario is addressed by formulating the game such that the Attacker is constrained to terminate at its Target, and the game value is the flyby distance. The latter is made accessible at termination by introducing an auxiliary state variable that records the players' minimum separation distance throughout their flight. This novel combination of termination condition and auxiliary state removes a structural limitation of current pursuit games and opens a new class of solvable problems. It also enables us to embed attacker mission constraints directly. Taking an indirect approach, we formulate the game's Two-Point Boundary Value Problem, solve it numerically, and identify several solution types. When the Attacker's only constraint is to reach the Target, he can do so without being captured. These types of solutions, however, are less practical due to long flight times and slow terminal speeds. Imposing an Attacker terminal speed constraint yields simpler solutions and reveals the trade-off between terminal speed and flyby distance. We demonstrate that in such a setting, capture is possible in a relevant region of the parameter space.

</details>


### [280] [A Survey of Community Detection from an Operations Research Perspective: Taxonomy, Mathematical Formulations, Modularity Functions, and Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13031)
*Miguel A. Pozo Montaño,Sebastián V Taboh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对网络科学中的社区检测问题进行了系统性综述，提出了一个统一的分析框架，重点研究了基于模块度的方法。文章构建了多维度分类体系，给出了社区检测问题的通用数学形式化定义，并系统回顾了代表性模块度函数、优化方法以及基准数据集，为研究人员和实践者提供了结构化参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社区检测综述缺乏统一的概念框架，分类标准各异，且对不同社区结构类型（不相交、重叠、模糊）缺乏统一的数学形式化描述。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用多维分类法（网络特征、社区结构、目标函数、方法论范式、评估标准、应用领域），提出了一个统一的社区检测问题数学形式化框架，涵盖不相交、重叠和模糊社区结构，并系统综述了模块度函数及其假设、零模型和局限性。

Result: 构建了一个集分类学、数学建模、模块度分析和基准测试于一体的结构化参考框架，区分了算法方法与数学规划方法两大技术路线。

Conclusion: 该综述为社区检测及相关的网络优化问题提供了系统化的参考资源，有助于研究者与实践者在统一框架下理解、选择和发展社区检测方法。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental problem in network science that consists of identifying groups of vertices exhibiting stronger internal connectivity than external connectivity. From an Operations Research perspective, the problem can be interpreted as a family of combinatorial optimization and clustering models defined over network structures. This survey provides a unified framework for the study of community detection, with particular emphasis on modularity-based approaches. We first review existing surveys and analyze the taxonomic criteria used to classify the literature, highlighting the absence of a common conceptual framework. Based on this analysis, we propose a multidimensional taxonomy that organizes community detection methods according to network characteristics, community structure, objective functions, methodological paradigms, evaluation criteria, and application domains. We then introduce a general mathematical formalization of the Community Detection Problem that accommodates disjoint, overlapping, and fuzzy community structures within a unified assignment framework. Building on this formalization, we review representative modularity functions, discussing their underlying assumptions, null models, and known limitations. We also survey modularity-based community detection methods, distinguishing between algorithmic and mathematical programming approaches. Finally, we review commonly used benchmark datasets and discuss their role in evaluation and reproducibility. By integrating taxonomy, mathematical modeling, modularity analysis, and benchmarking practices, this survey provides a structured reference for researchers and practitioners working on community detection and related network optimization problems.

</details>


### [281] [Scheduling of Star Observations under Uncertain Conditions: A Comparison of Models and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13058)
*Thomas Rahab Lacroix,Pierre Lemaire,Nadia Brauner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究望远镜观测恒星的调度问题，将夜晚视为机器、恒星视为作业，在考虑气象不确定性（夜晚可能完美或完全不可用）的情况下，采用鲁棒优化范式评估最坏情况，目标是最大化观测总利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际恒星观测调度中，气象和大气条件在制定计划时未知，需要在不确知最终可用夜晚数量的情况下生成高质量调度方案。

Method: 将问题建模为并行机调度，每个夜晚有两种状态（完美或完全不可用），采用鲁棒优化方法评估最坏情况下的调度性能。

Result: 给出了在夜晚数量不确定条件下的鲁棒调度方案及其最坏情况性能评估。

Conclusion: 鲁棒优化框架能有效处理恒星观测中气象不确定性带来的调度挑战，为实际望远镜观测提供理论保障。

Abstract: We consider a scheduling problem for star observations using a telescope. Identical nights are available to observe stars that have been identified as of interest by astronomers. Each star yields a profit if it is observed; in that case, the observation must respect a visibility window and a minimum observation duration. The objective is to maximize the total profit of the observations that are actually performed. By interpreting nights as machines and stars as jobs to be processed, this problem is a classic scheduling setting of parallel machines. In the practical star-observation problem, the ability to observe depends on meteorological and atmospheric conditions, which are not known when the schedule is computed. A possible modeling is to consider that each night can be either perfect and everything is observable, either terrible and nothing is observable. The challenge is therefore to propose a ''high-quality'' schedule without knowing the actual number of nights (machines) that will ultimately be available. We focus here on evaluating the worst case within the robust optimization paradigm.

</details>


### [282] [A symmetric Gauss-Seidel based alternating proximal ALM for generalized Nash Equilibrium problems in Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13198)
*Defeng Sun,Hailing Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有联合线性约束的单调广义纳什均衡问题(GNEPs)，提出了一种基于对称高斯-赛德尔(sGS)的交替邻近增广拉格朗日方法(sGS-APALM)，该方法通过设计新的二次替代函数，交替更新玩家策略，避免了求解耦合子问题，且仅需伪梯度映射的单调性和Lipschitz连续性即可保证全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的正则化和ALM类方法在求解GNEP时需要在每次迭代中求解耦合的纳什均衡子问题，计算代价高昂；而现有分裂方法往往需要强单调性或共强制性等更强假设。因此需要一种既能避免求解耦合子问题，又能在较弱的单调性假设下保证收敛的高效算法。

Method: 提出sGS-APALM方法：采用对称高斯-赛德尔技术设计的交替邻近增广拉格朗日框架，引入新设计的二次替代函数，将原始耦合问题分解为一系列无约束二次规划子问题，各玩家策略交替更新。

Result: 全局收敛性分析和收敛速率估计仅需伪梯度映射的单调性和Lipschitz连续性，无需强单调性或共强制性等更强假设。在风险中性PDE约束的GNEPs上的初步数值实验验证了方法的效率和有效性。

Conclusion: sGS-APALM为求解一类单调GNEPs提供了一种高效、可分解的替代方案，在较弱的假设下实现了全局收敛，具有较好的理论保证和实际应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a class of monotone generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) with jointly linear constraints. The players' strategy spaces are real Hilbert spaces, while the joint constraint is formulated in a Banach space. To solve such problems, we propose a novel symmetric Gauss-Seidel (sGS) based alternating proximal augmented Lagrangian method (sGS-APALM) which incorporates newly designed quadratic surrogates. In contrast to existing regularization and ALM-type methods, the proposed method avoids solving coupled Nash equilibrium subproblems at each iteration and instead updates the players' strategies alternately by solving a sequence of unconstrained quadratic programs. Moreover, unlike many existing splitting-based methods, our global convergence analysis and convergence rate estimation require only monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the pseudo-gradient mapping, without imposing stronger assumptions such as strong monotonicity or co-coercivity. Finally, we apply the method to a class of risk-neutral PDE-constrained GNEPs with joint state constraints, and preliminary numerical results demonstrate its efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [283] [A smoothing extended sequential quadratic method for difference-of-convex optimization over a convex composite inequality constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13343)
*Jiefeng Xu,Ting Kei Pong,Yongle Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对差凸目标函数在凸复合不等式约束和紧凸集约束下的优化问题，提出了结合可变平滑机制的ESQM扩展算法。每次迭代对平滑罚函数执行一步近端梯度更新，并设计平滑参数和罚参数的自适应更新规则。在合适约束规格下，算法达到(ε,ε)-KKT点的迭代复杂度为O(ε⁻³)；凸情形下证明了全序列收敛性，并在Hölderian增长条件下导出了局部收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决差凸目标函数在复合凸约束和紧凸集约束下的优化问题，现有ESQM方法未考虑平滑机制，需要更高效的算法。

Method: 将可变平滑方案融入ESQM框架：基于凸复合约束函数的平滑近似构造平滑罚函数，每轮迭代执行一步近端梯度更新，并设计平滑参数和罚参数的显式更新规则。

Result: 在约束规格下，算法达到(ε,ε)-KKT点的迭代复杂度为O(ε⁻³)；凸情形下全序列收敛，且在标准Hölderian增长条件下得到局部收敛速率。

Conclusion: 所提算法有效求解差凸优化问题，具有理论保证的迭代复杂度和收敛性质。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing a difference-of-convex objective over a convex composite inequality constraint and a compact convex set constraint. To solve this problem, we extend the ESQM in [1] via incorporating a variable smoothing scheme. In essence, in each iteration of our algorithm, we apply one proximal gradient step to a smoothed penalty function, constructed based on a smooth approximation of the convex composite constraint function; and we design explicit rules to update the smoothing and penalty parameters. Under suitable constraint qualifications, we establish an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for obtaining an $(ε,ε)$-KKT point. Moreover, in the convex setting, we show that the whole sequence generated by our algorithm is convergent and derive its local convergence rate under a standard Hölderian growth condition.

</details>


### [284] [Coordinate-wise Polyhedral Method for Eliciting Multivariate Linear Utility and Univariate Nonlinear Utility Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13418)
*Jiaxin Wei,Jia Liu,Huifu Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种坐标方向多面体方法（CPM），通过预先指定坐标方向切割并求解线性方程组来设计成对比较查询，实现了多面体直径的线性收敛速率，并扩展至分段线性和非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数的估计，理论分析与数值实验均验证了其收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多面体方法需要求解耦合优化问题来设计成对比较查询，计算成本高；CPM旨在通过预先指定坐标方向切割来简化查询设计过程，同时保持理论上的收敛保证。

Method: CPM预先指定坐标方向切割，通过求解线性方程组设计对应的成对比较查询；对于单变量分段线性效用函数，利用增量向量表示；对于一般非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数，采用分段线性近似（PLA）并引入自适应断点策略以避免方向误差。

Result: CPM实现多面体直径的线性收敛速率；对于分段线性效用函数，Kantorovich距离以线性速率下降；对于一般非线性函数，增量向量对应的模糊集收敛到真实效用函数，并给出了显式逼近误差界；数值实验验证了与标准多面体方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: CPM作为一种新颖的切割多面体方法，在保证线性收敛率的同时简化了查询设计过程，并能有效推广到非线性效用函数的估计问题，具有理论和实践上的应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a coordinate-wise polyhedral method (CPM) for cutting polyhedra with theoretical guarantees of convergence. Unlike the existing polyhedral method, which designs pairwise comparison queries by solving coupled optimization problems and performs cuts subsequently, CPM specifies coordinate-wise cuts in advance and then designs corresponding pairwise comparison queries by solving a linear system of equations. Under this framework, we show that CPM reduces the diameter of the polyhedron at a linear convergence rate. Moreover, we extend CPM to a univariate piecewise-linear utility function by representing it with its increment vector over consecutive breakpoints. We show that the Kantorovich distance between the true utility function and the estimated one obtained by CPM decreases at a linear convergence rate. Further, we extend the CPM to general nondecreasing Lipschitz continuous utility functions by piecewise-linear approximation (PLA). We introduce an adaptive-breakpoint strategy to avoid direction errors caused by PLA. We prove that the set of piecewise-linear utility functions corresponding to the ambiguity set of increment vectors converges to the true utility function as the number of queries increases, and derive an explicit bound for the approximation. Finally, to evaluate the performance of CPM, we conduct a series of numerical experiments. The results demonstrate comparable convergence to the standard polyhedral method in the case of linear multivariate utility functions. For nonlinear univariate utility functions, CPM achieves stable convergence to the true utility function, in line with the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [285] [Towards a Control interpretation of Quantum Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13481)
*Dario Pighin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个控制论框架来理解量子优势（QA），通过将量子计算建模为特殊酉群上的算子可控性问题，并用最小时间函数的多项式上界来刻画QA。框架在量子傅里叶变换（超导数字量子处理器）和最大独立集问题（中性原子模拟量子处理器）两个范例上得到验证，分别证明了算子可控性并推导了最小时间上界。最后提出了控制理论与量子计算交叉领域的若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一个系统的控制论框架来表征量子优势何时以及如何产生，弥补现有研究中缺乏统一理论工具的不足。

Method: 将量子计算重述为双线性受控薛定谔方程下的算子可控性问题，利用李代数论证证明可控性，通过门级联引理和标准电路分解推导最小时间上界，并将量子近似优化算法重述为连续时间最优控制问题。

Result: 对QFT证明了算子可控性并推导了O(n²)最小时间上界；对MIS问题证明了可控性并提出了基于控制的量子优势定义。

Conclusion: 控制论框架为理解量子优势提供了系统化路径，验证了其在数字和模拟量子处理器上的适用性，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: We develop a control-theoretic framework for understanding Quantum Advantage (QA), providing a systematic route to characterize when and how QA can arise. The bilinear controlled Schrödinger equation is the common thread: the target quantum computation is recast as an operator controllability problem on the special unitary group $SU(N)$, and QA is identified with a polynomial-in-$n$ upper bound on the associated minimal-time function.
  We illustrate the framework on two paradigmatic problems: a) the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT) on superconducting digital quantum processors (such as IBM's ibm_brisbane), for which we prove operator controllability by a Lie-algebraic argument and derive an $O(n^2)$ upper bound on the minimal time via a gate-concatenation lemma combined with the standard QFT circuit decomposition; b) the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem on neutral-atom analog quantum processors (such as Pasqal's hardware), for which we analyze the Rydberg-blockade Hamiltonian as a bilinear control system and reformulate the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) as a continuous-time optimal control problem. By a controllability result, we show how the problem can be solved on Pasqal Quantum Computers and we introduce a control-based definition of Quantum Advantage for MIS.
  We conclude by outlining several open problems that chart directions for future research at the intersection of Control Theory and Quantum Computing.

</details>


### [286] [A Sustainable Integrated Framework for Multi-Type Urban Waste Collection and Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13524)
*Víctor Blanco,J. Fernando Camacho-Vallejo,Yolanda Hinojosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多类型城市垃圾收集与回收系统的集成优化框架，通过联合确定集中设施和处理厂的位置及两层级路径规划，在有限基础设施预算下最小化运营总成本。基于哥伦比亚麦德林市的实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市垃圾管理面临人口增长、垃圾种类多样、交通拥堵和可持续收集基础设施需求等日益严峻的挑战，亟需一种能够同时优化设施选址与路径规划的集成方案。

Method: 构建集成选址-路径模型，同时捕获设施选址决策、多产品路径结构、车辆容量限制和重复卸载操作，目标是最小化包含运输、路径规划和处理活动的总运营成本，并满足投资预算约束。

Result: 基于哥伦比亚麦德林市真实场景的计算实验表明，集成式基础设施与路径决策能够显著提升运营效率，支持更可持续的回收作业。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为城市规划者设计经济高效且环境友好的垃圾管理系统的实用决策支持工具。

Abstract: Urban waste management faces increasing operational and environmental challenges driven by population growth, heterogeneous waste streams, traffic congestion, and the need for sustainable collection infrastructures. We present an integrated optimization framework for the design of multi-type urban waste collection and recycling systems. In the proposed network, waste generated at distributed collection points is gathered by waste-specific small vehicles and transported to intermediate consolidation facilities, where it is aggregated before being transferred to treatment plants using larger vehicles. The problem consists of jointly determining the location of consolidation facilities and treatment plants, together with the associated two-echelon routing operations, under a limited infrastructure budget. We develop an integrated location-routing model that simultaneously captures facility-location decisions, multi-product routing structures, vehicle-capacity restrictions, and repeated unloading operations. The objective is to minimize total operational costs, including transportation, routing, and handling activities, while satisfying investment-budget constraints. The framework promotes compact collection regions, improves vehicle utilization, and reduces unnecessary transportation effort in dense urban environments. Computational experiments based on realistic urban scenarios derived from the city of Medellín (Colombia) demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. The results show that integrated infrastructure and routing decisions can substantially improve operational efficiency while supporting more sustainable recycling operations. The framework provides a practical decision-support tool for urban planners seeking to design cost-efficient and environmentally responsible waste-management systems.

</details>


### [287] [Differential Geometric Conditions for Koopman Linearizability of Control-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13577)
*Shankar A. Deka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性控制系统的Koopman线性化问题，提出了控制仿射系统存在Koopman线性变换的微分几何必要条件，证明了这些条件在控制不变流形上对于弱化Koopman线性化的充分性，并给出了达到可控线性系统的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定非线性控制系统是否允许有限维Koopman表示是一个关键问题，需要对所有Koopman可线性化的非线性控制系统进行分类，本文旨在给出可线性化的微分几何条件。

Method: 采用微分几何方法，分析控制仿射系统的漂移向量场和控制向量场，推导Koopman线性变换存在的必要条件，并在控制不变流形上证明其充分性。

Result: 给出了控制仿射系统Koopman线性化的必要条件，证明了在控制不变流形上这些条件对于弱化Koopman线性化是充分的，加上额外条件后可达到可控线性系统的充要条件。

Conclusion: 即使系统的自治部分可以通过Koopman提升线性化，控制仿射系统也可能不存在Koopman线性化变换，本文的条件易于检验。

Abstract: Koopman linearization opens many possibilities for control synthesis and analysis of nonlinear systems. Whether or not any given nonlinear control system admits a finite-dimensional Koopman representation remains a crucial question to address. A related problem is to categorize the class of all Koopman linearizable nonlinear control systems. In this work, we present differential geometric conditions on the drift and control vector fields of a control-affine nonlinear system, that must be necessarily satisfied for Koopman linear transformation to exist. The same conditions are also shown to be sufficient for (a slightly weaker notion of) Koopman linearizability on control-invariant manifolds. Further, these conditions, together with an additional condition, become necessary and sufficient for Koopman linearizability to a controllable linear system. Our examples illustrate the ease of checking these conditions, and also shed light on how Koopman linearizing transformation may not exist for a control-affine system even though one can linearize the autonomous part of the system via Koopman lifting.

</details>


### [288] [Optimal Proximity Bound and Product Function Estimates in Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13579)
*Iskander Aliev,Gennadiy Averkov,William Jones,Timm Oertel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为整数线性规划标准形式（max{cx: Ax=b, x≥0整数}）建立了最优邻近性界：LP松弛最优顶点解到最近整数最优解的欧氏距离不超过√det(AA^t)-1，且该界渐近紧致；同时给出了涉及乘积函数∏(x_i+1)的整数解界，并讨论了在背包问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数线性规划（ILP）中，LP松弛的最优解与整数最优解之间的邻近性（proximity）是理解整数规划求解难度和解质量的核心问题。已有邻近性界往往依赖于数据规模或维数，本文旨在建立仅依赖于矩阵A的几何性质（det(AA^t)）的通用最优邻近性界，并证明其紧致性。

Method: 利用线性规划对偶理论、整数格点的几何性质（如Minkowski定理及其变体），结合矩阵的秩与行列式分析，推导LP最优顶点解与整数最优解之间的Euclidean距离上界；通过构造具体实例证明该界是渐近紧致的。对于乘积函数界，采用类似的技术框架并应用于背包问题。

Result: 1) LP松弛最优顶点解到最近整数最优解的Euclidean距离≤√det(AA^t)-1；2) 该界是渐近紧致的（存在实例使距离任意接近该上界）；3) 给出了涉及∏(x_i+1)的整数解界，并讨论了其在背包问题中的具体含义和应用。

Conclusion: 建立了整数线性规划中一个依赖于det(AA^t)的最优邻近性界，该界不依赖于问题规模参数（如系数大小、右端项大小），具有几何直观性且渐近最优。乘积函数界为背包问题等特殊结构的ILP提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We obtain an optimal proximity bound for integer linear programs in standard form max{cx: Ax=b, x nonnegative integer}, where A is an integer mxn matrix of rank m<n and b is an integer vector. Specifically, we show that the Euclidean distance from any optimal vertex solution of the LP relaxation to a nearest optimal integer solution is bounded by $\sqrt{\det(AA^t)}-1$ and that this estimate is asymptotically tight. We also derive bounds for the optimal integer solutions involving the product function $\prod_{i=1}^{n}(x_i+1)$ and discuss their applications in the knapsack setting.

</details>


### [289] [Distribution-Agnostic Robust Trajectory Optimization via Chance-Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13605)
*Yashdeep Chaudhary,Roberto Armellin,Harry Holt,Marco Sagliano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机会约束强化学习的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化框架。该框架先离线计算确定性标称轨迹，再通过由前馈控制调整和时变反馈增益组成的仿射闭环修正律进行鲁棒化，利用 rollout 上尾分位数强制执行概率可行性，并通过协方差可行性惩罚约束终端散布。在三维多脉冲地火转移和随机大气动力火箭着陆两个案例上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒轨迹优化方法通常假设不确定性服从特定分布（如高斯分布），缺乏分布无关性。此外，传统方法在处理高维、非线性和复杂约束的航天器轨迹设计问题时计算负担重，且难以将同一鲁棒化框架迁移至不同任务场景。

Method: 采用离线-在线两阶段策略：离线阶段计算确定性标称轨迹；在线阶段利用强化学习学习仿射闭环修正律（前馈调整+时变反馈增益），通过 rollout 上尾分位数经验性地强制执行概率可行性，并引入协方差可行性惩罚来调节终端散布。不确定性仅需可采样，无需假设具体分布。

Result: 在三维多脉冲地火转移任务中，该方法在燃料成本上尾分位数方面与先进鲁棒优化参考方法竞争力相当，同时保持了概率可行性；在均匀不确定性和未训练的随机过程扰动下仍表现良好。在随机大气火箭着陆问题中，该方法也成功应用于短时域连续推力场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化框架能够在保证概率可行性的同时保持燃料成本竞争力，且同一鲁棒化结构可跨不同航天器轨迹规划问题迁移，无需重新设计核心随机控制结构。

Abstract: This paper presents a distribution-agnostic robust trajectory-optimization framework based on chance-constrained reinforcement learning. The uncertainty is represented here through initial conditions and process noise, with the only requirement being that it can be sampled. A deterministic nominal trajectory is first computed offline, and reinforcement learning is then used only to robustify that baseline through a structured affine closed-loop correction law comprising a feedforward control adjustment and time-varying feedback gains. Probabilistic feasibility is enforced empirically through rollout-based upper-tail quantiles, while terminal dispersion is regulated through covariance-feasibility penalties. The framework is assessed on two materially different trajectory design problems. The flagship case study is a three-dimensional multi-impulse Earth-Mars transfer, where the learned policy is benchmarked against a recent robust trajectory-optimization reference under Gaussian uncertainty and then evaluated under bounded uniform uncertainty and under process disturbances not seen during training. The second case study is a stochastic atmospheric pinpoint rocket landing problem, used to assess portability to a short-horizon continuous-thrust setting with drag, mass depletion, and glide-slope constraints. The results show that the proposed framework can remain competitive in upper-tail fuel cost while preserving probabilistic feasibility, and that the same robustification scaffold can be carried across heterogeneous spacecraft trajectory planning problems without redesign of its core stochastic-control structure.

</details>
